_Audit log (pass 3, 2026-07-06): Refines pass-2's open items. (a) The latent process-wide culture race noted for UnitTestBase in UniversalDeviceToolkit.Tests/Infrastructure/TestBase.cs:33-43 is structurally MITIGATED: the test assembly carries [assembly: CollectionBehavior(DisableTestParallelization = true)] at UniversalDeviceToolkit.Tests/AssemblyInfo.cs:3, so xUnit never runs its tests concurrently; the static Culture swaps there cannot race. Not filed. (b) SkipException-misuse audit: opencode debug rg search 'throw new SkipException' --glob *.cs returned only the 3 base-class throw-sites in FlaUiTestBase.cs:80,109,114 (all in the shared base InitializeAsync, none in test method bodies) - no second instance of the [UDT-005] [Fact]-vs-[SkippableFact] defect; UDT-005 remains the only such ticket. UDT-001..004 stay archived; UDT-005 stays open. UniversalDeviceToolkit.Tests full dotnet test not re-run this pass (no code delta since pass-2; pass-2 result: 2326 pass / 3 fail / 27 skip of 2356)._

- [ ] **[UDT-020]** [Resource Leak] Process handle leak in ProcessAutoListener.cs:86,144. *Root Cause*: Line 86 `Process.GetProcessById(e.ProcessId).GetFileName()` creates a Process object without `using` 闁?the handle leaks to GC finalizer on every new-process event. Line 144 `_ = Process.GetProcessById(processId)` creates a Process for existence-check validation only, leaks handle on every cache cleanup iteration. Both paths catch ArgumentException but never dispose the Process on the success path. *Suggested Fix*: Wrap in `using`: `using var process = Process.GetProcessById(e.ProcessId); processPath = process.GetFileName();` and `using var p = Process.GetProcessById(processId);`

_Audit log (pass 4, 2026-07-06): [UDT-005] CLOSED (FlaUIMainWindowTests switched [Fact] -> [SkippableFact] with MainWindow! null-forgiving; driver suite 0 failed / 3 skipped; full suite 2327 passed / 0 failed / 30 skipped; root cause + rule transcribed to KNOWLEDGE_BASE.md entry + Principle #11). Brand-migration audit (per maintainer note that anything named LenovoLegionToolkit needs attention because the product scope widened to UniversalDeviceToolkit) classified every LenovoLegionToolkit reference into user-facing brand vs cross-repo ABI identifier. It found THREE real user-facing leaks beyond an earlier "zero literal" claim: (a) FpsSensorController.InitializeBlacklist excluded only the legacy process name so the renamed Universal Device Toolkit self-window was counted as a monitored app; (b) HWiNFOIntegration.CUSTOM_SENSOR_GROUP_NAME still registered HWiNFO custom sensors under the old Lenovo Legion Toolkit registry group; (c) Plugin Workbench HostResourceLookup.ResolveResourceType matched only the legacy host assembly name and failed under a UDT-named host. All three fixed by pairing the legacy literal with the new name (and cleaning the legacy HWiNFO key on stop); full main solution build => 0 errors / 0 warnings, full plugin solution build (TreatWarningsAsErrors) => 0 errors / 0 warnings, full test suite => 0 failures. Remaining LenovoLegionToolkit.* references are confirmed ABI identifiers (Lib <AssemblyName>, plugin SDK namespaces, clr-namespace) intentionally PRESERVED as cross-repo load contracts, plus intentional legacy/dual-name constants (AppIdentity.LegacyDisplayName, ProcessAutoListener blacklist, HWiNFOIntegration.LEGACY_CUSTOM_SENSOR_GROUP_NAME). No new ticket opened - this was maintenance evolution found by audit, not an externally-reported defect; recorded in CHANGELOG [Unreleased] Fixed and KNOWLEDGE_BASE Principle #12. No queue changes; 4_ARCHIVED.md already holds UDT-001..005 + PLG-001..002; 1_NEW_REPORTS.md has no open tickets._

## UDT-011..018 (Filed 2026-07-07, Codex-Maintenance)

- [ ] **[UDT-011]** [Resource Leak] CancellationTokenSource not disposed on replacement in MacroPlayer.cs:63. *Root Cause*: _cancellationTokenSource = new() after CancelAsync() but never .Dispose() 闂?the old CTS's internal timer/registration resources leak until GC finalizer runs. *Suggested Fix*: ar old = _cancellationTokenSource; _cancellationTokenSource = new(); old?.Dispose();

- [ ] **[UDT-012]** [Resource Leak] CancellationTokenSource not disposed on replacement in FpsSensorController.cs:75. *Root Cause*: _cancellationTokenSource = new CancellationTokenSource() in StartMonitoringAsync() without disposing the prior instance; repeated calls leak CTS objects. *Suggested Fix*: _cancellationTokenSource?.Dispose(); _cancellationTokenSource = new CancellationTokenSource();

- [ ] **[UDT-013]** [Resource Leak] CancellationTokenSource not disposed on replacement in DeviceAutomationPipelineTriggerTabItemContent.xaml.cs:84. *Root Cause*: _filterDebounceCancellationTokenSource = new() after CancelAsync() without disposing the old CTS. Debounce-triggered text changes leak CTS on every keystroke burst. *Suggested Fix*: _filterDebounceCancellationTokenSource?.Dispose(); _filterDebounceCancellationTokenSource = new();

- [ ] **[UDT-014]** [Resource Leak] CancellationTokenSource not disposed on replacement in SpectrumKeyboardBacklightControl.xaml.cs:440,453. *Root Cause*: Two reassignment sites (_refreshStateCancellationTokenSource = new()) cancel-then-replace without disposing the old CTS. Lines 129-135 show the correct dispose pattern is known but not applied at L440/L453. *Suggested Fix*: _refreshStateCancellationTokenSource?.Dispose(); _refreshStateCancellationTokenSource = new();

- [ ] **[UDT-015]** [Threading] GetAwaiter().GetResult() in AIController.Dispose(bool):302 闂?potential deadlock. *Root Cause*: gameAutoListener.UnsubscribeChangedAsync(...).GetAwaiter().GetResult() blocks the Dispose thread. If the unsubscription requires UI thread (e.g., dispatcher marshal), this deadlocks the finalizer/GC thread. *Suggested Fix*: Fire-and-forget via _ = Task.Run(async () => await gameAutoListener.UnsubscribeChangedAsync(...)) or convert to async dispose (IAsyncDisposable).

- [ ] **[UDT-016]** [Hook Teardown] KB#13 violation in MacroRecorder.cs:89-108 闂?unhook before field clear. *Root Cause*: PInvoke.UnhookWindowsHookEx(_kbHook) and _mouseHook called while fields still hold the handle; fields cleared only at L110-111 after both unhook calls. If a hook callback fires during unhook, it sees a stale handle. *Suggested Fix*: Capture to local, clear field, then unhook: ar h = _kbHook; _kbHook = default; PInvoke.UnhookWindowsHookEx(h);

- [ ] **[UDT-017]** [Hook Teardown] KB#13 violation in NativeWindowsMessageListener.cs:96-139 闂?unhook/unregister before field clear. *Root Cause*: UnhookWindowsHookEx(_kbHook) and 4x UnregisterPower* calls precede field clearing at L136+. All handles are still live in fields during teardown, risking stale-handle callback races. *Suggested Fix*: Capture each handle to a local, clear the field to default, then call the unhook/unregister API with the local.

- [ ] **[UDT-018]** [Hook Teardown] KB#13 violation in UserInactivityAutoListener.cs:57-61 闂?unhook before field clear. *Root Cause*: UnhookWindowsHookEx(_kbHook) and _mouseHook called, then _kbHook = HHOOK.Null; _mouseHook = HHOOK.Null; follows. Same stale-handle race as UDT-016/017. *Suggested Fix*: ar kb = _kbHook; var ms = _mouseHook; _kbHook = default; _mouseHook = default; PInvoke.UnhookWindowsHookEx(kb); PInvoke.UnhookWindowsHookEx(ms);

- [ ] **[UDT-021]** [Build Break] Test file AppIdentityTests.cs references non-existent AppIdentity class. *Root Cause*: UniversalDeviceToolkit.Tests/AppIdentityTests.cs (lines 60-109) references AppIdentity.DisplayName, AppIdentity.CompactName, AppIdentity.LegacyDisplayName etc. but no AppIdentity class exists in any production assembly.
